Concatenate all text content\n                full_text = ''.join(t.text or '' for t in text_nodes)\n\n                # Check if this paragraph contains any placeholders\n                if '{{' not in full_text:\n                    continue\n\n                # Replace all placeholders in the concatenated text\n                new_text = full_text\n                for key, value in replacements.items():\n                    placeholder = f'{{{{{key}}}}}'\n                    new_text = new_text.replace(placeholder, value)\n\n                # Clear all text nodes except the first one\n                for t in text_nodes[1:]:\n                    t.text = ''\n\n                # Set the first text node's text to the fully replaced string\n                if text_nodes:\n                    text_nodes[0].text = new_text\n                    # Clear other text nodes completely (remove their text attribute)\n                    for t in text_nodes[1:]:\n                        parent = t.getparent()\n                        if parent is not None:\n                            parent.remove(t)\n\n                # Remove lineSegArray elements - THIS IS CRITICAL\n                # Check both lowercase and camelCase versions\n                for lineseg_tag in ['linesegarray', 'lineSegArray']:\n                    for ls in para.findall(f'hp:{lineseg_tag}', NSMAP):\n                        para.remove(ls)\n\n                modified = True\n\n            if modified:\n                # Serialize the modified XML\n                new_content = etree.tostring(root, encoding='utf-8', xml_declaration=True)\n                file_contents[filename] = new_content\n                modified_files.add(filename)\n                print(f\"Modified: {filename}\")\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Error processing {filename}: {e}\")\n\nprint(f\"\\nModified files: {modified_files}\")\n\n# Create the output HWPX file\nwith zipfile.ZipFile(output_path, 'w', zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zout:\n    for filename in file_list:\n        zout.writestr(filename, file_contents[filename])\n\nprint(f\"\\nOutput written to: {output_path}\")\n\n# Validation\nprint(\"\\n=== Validation ===\")\nwith zipfile.ZipFile(output_path, 'r') as z:\n    for filename in modified_files:\n        content = z.read(filename).decode('utf-8')\n\n        # Serialize the modified XML\n                new_content = etree.tostring(root, encoding='utf-8', xml_declaration=True)\n                file_contents[filename] = new_content\n                modified_files.add(filename)\n                print(f\"Modified: {filename}\")\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Error processing {filename}: {e}\")\n            import traceback\n            traceback.print_exc()\n\nprint(f\"\\nModified files: {modified_files}\")\n\n# Create the output HWPX file\nwith zipfile.ZipFile(output_path, 'w', zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zout:\n    for filename in file_list:\n        zout.writestr(filename, file_contents[filename])\n\nprint(f\"\\nOutput written to: {output_path}\")\n\n# Validation\nprint(\"\\n=== Validation ===\")\nwith zipfile.ZipFile(output_path, 'r') as z:\n    for filename in modified_files:\n        content = z.read(filename).decode('utf-8')\n        print(f\"\\n--- {filename} ---\")\n\n        #
